File directory: services-integration-assistance-cases-facade/apps/serviceIntegrationAssistanceCasesFacade/common/callable_SetContext_Compute.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE callable_SetContext_Compute
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;

		-- copy all in the LocalEnv as it is passed to the callable flow
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Correlation-Id" = Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context;
		SET Environment."X-Caller-Code" = ApplicationLabel;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Environment = Environment;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Caller-Code" = ApplicationLabel;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyMessageHeaders() BEGIN
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		DECLARE J INTEGER;
		SET J = CARDINALITY(InputRoot.*[]);
		WHILE I < J DO
			SET OutputRoot.*[I] = InputRoot.*[I];
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyEntireMessage() BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
	END;
END MODULE;

================================================================================

File directory: services-integration-assistance-cases-facade/apps/serviceIntegrationAssistanceCasesFacade/common/Functions.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common

CREATE FUNCTION checkIdReferenceCaseFormat(IN idReference CHARACTER) RETURNS BOOLEAN
BEGIN
	IF COALESCE(idReference, '') <> '' AND LENGTH(idReference) = 9 AND CAST(idReference AS INT DEFAULT NULL) IS NOT NULL
	THEN
		RETURN TRUE;
	END IF;
	RETURN FALSE;
END;

================================================================================

File directory: services-integration-assistance-cases-facade/apps/serviceIntegrationAssistanceCasesFacade/common/setReplyStatusCode_Compute.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE setReplyStatusCode_Compute
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;

		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = COALESCE(InputRoot.JSON.Data.httpCode, COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestIdentifier,'500'));
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyMessageHeaders() BEGIN
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		DECLARE J INTEGER;
		SET J = CARDINALITY(InputRoot.*[]);
		WHILE I < J DO
			SET OutputRoot.*[I] = InputRoot.*[I];
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyEntireMessage() BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: services-integration-assistance-cases-facade/apps/serviceIntegrationAssistanceCasesFacade/create_case_IsAssys_Filter.esql

PATH Shlib_PoliciesReader;

CREATE FILTER MODULE create_case_IsAssys_Filter
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE sfcasActivated CHARACTER gestionPolicy('{services-integration-policies-credentials-sfcas}:SfcasCredentialsPolicy', 'sfcas-activated');

		IF UPPER(COALESCE(sfcasActivated, '')) = 'FALSE'
		THEN
			RETURN TRUE;
		ELSE
			RETURN FALSE;
		END IF;
	END;

END MODULE;

File directory: services-integration-assistance-cases-facade/apps/serviceIntegrationAssistanceCasesFacade/createUpdate_service_IsAssys_Filter.esql

PATH Shlib_PoliciesReader;

CREATE FILTER MODULE createUpdate_service_IsAssys_Filter
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE sfcasActivated CHARACTER gestionPolicy('{services-integration-policies-credentials-sfcas}:SfcasCredentialsPolicy', 'sfcas-activated');

		IF UPPER(COALESCE(sfcasActivated, '')) = 'FALSE' OR common.checkIdReferenceCaseFormat(LocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Parameters."case-id") = TRUE
		THEN
			RETURN TRUE;
		ELSE
			RETURN FALSE;
		END IF;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: services-integration-assistance-cases-facade/apps/serviceIntegrationAssistanceCasesFacade/field_agent_event_PrepareResponse_Compute.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE field_agent_event_PrepareResponse_Compute
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot.Properties = InputRoot.Properties;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyMessageHeaders() BEGIN
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		DECLARE J INTEGER;
		SET J = CARDINALITY(InputRoot.*[]);
		WHILE I < J DO
			SET OutputRoot.*[I] = InputRoot.*[I];
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyEntireMessage() BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
	END;
END MODULE;

================================================================================

File directory: services-integration-assistance-cases-facade/apps/serviceIntegrationAssistanceCasesFacade/field_agent_event_SetBusinessIdentifier_Compute.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE field_agent_event_SetBusinessIdentifier_Compute
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;

		-- Set business identifier
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.businessIdentifier.Info.caseId = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Parameters."case-id";
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.businessIdentifier.Info.serviceId = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Parameters."service-delivery-id";
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.businessIdentifier.Info.fieldAgentId = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Parameters."field-agent-id";

		-- Store parameter
		SET Environment.Variables.REST = InputLocalEnvironment.REST;

		-- copy all in the LocalEnv as it is passed to the callable flow
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Correlation-Id" = Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Environment = Environment;

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyMessageHeaders() BEGIN
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		DECLARE J INTEGER;
		SET J = CARDINALITY(InputRoot.*[]);
		WHILE I < J DO
			SET OutputRoot.*[I] = InputRoot.*[I];
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyEntireMessage() BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: services-integration-assistance-cases-facade/apps/serviceIntegrationAssistanceCasesFacade/history_case_CheckInputParameters.esql



CREATE COMPUTE MODULE history_case_CheckInputParameters
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		
		if LENGTH(COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Parameters.personalreference,'')) = 0 or 
		   LENGTH(COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Parameters.personalreference,'')) > 9 or
		   cast(InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Parameters.personalreference as INT DEFAULT 1000000000) >= 1000000000
		then
		
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-code" = 400;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-message" = 'Bad Request';
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-stack" = 'The parameter personalreference does not respect the expected format.';
			propagate to terminal 1;
			return false;
		end if;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

================================================================================

File directory: services-integration-assistance-cases-facade/apps/serviceIntegrationAssistanceCasesFacade/MessageFilter_FilterFSMMessage.esql



CREATE FILTER MODULE MessageFilter_FilterFSMMessage
	-- Return TRUE if the FSM is for FSM (blank or MLOG value in Consumer-Value)
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE returnValue BOOLEAN;
		
		SET returnValue = FALSE;
		IF EXISTS(Root.JSON.Data.ContextList[]) THEN
			FOR contextElement AS Root.JSON.Data.ContextList.Item[] DO
				IF contextElement.ContextElementTypeKey = 'X-CONSUMER-NAME' AND 
					(COALESCE(contextElement.ContextElementValue, '') = '' OR contextElement.ContextElementValue = 'MLOG') THEN
					SET returnValue = TRUE;
				END IF;    
			END FOR;
		END IF;
		RETURN returnValue;
	END;

END MODULE;

File directory: services-integration-assistance-cases-facade/apps/serviceIntegrationAssistanceCasesFacade/update_case_IsAssys_Filter.esql

PATH Shlib_PoliciesReader;

CREATE FILTER MODULE update_case_IsAssys_Filter
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE sfcasActivated CHARACTER gestionPolicy('{services-integration-policies-credentials-sfcas}:SfcasCredentialsPolicy', 'sfcas-activated');
		DECLARE paylaodRef REFERENCE TO Root.JSON.Data;

		IF UPPER(COALESCE(sfcasActivated, '')) = 'FALSE' OR checkTCSReference(paylaodRef) = TRUE OR common.checkIdReferenceCaseFormat(paylaodRef.Id) = TRUE
		THEN
			RETURN TRUE;
		ELSE
			RETURN FALSE;
		END IF;
	END;

	CREATE FUNCTION checkTCSReference(IN payloadRef REFERENCE) RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		DECLARE caseItemRef REFERENCE TO payloadRef.CaseReferenceList.Item[1];

		WHILE LASTMOVE(caseItemRef)
	    DO
	   		IF UPPER(caseItemRef.Organisation.Name) = 'TCS' AND common.checkIdReferenceCaseFormat(caseItemRef.Reference)
	   		THEN
	   			RETURN TRUE;
	   		END IF;
			MOVE caseItemRef NEXTSIBLING REPEAT TYPE NAME;
		END WHILE;

		RETURN FALSE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: services-integration-shlib-error-functions/apps/SHLIB_CommonErrorFunctions/CommonHelper/BuildErrorAndReturn500.esql

BROKER SCHEMA CommonHelper

-- return code. May be change using the property of the subflow
DECLARE errorcode EXTERNAL INTEGER 500;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE BuildErrorAndReturn500
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		CALL CopyMessageHeaders();
		
		-- an error has been trapped and passed in LocalEnvironment
		IF EXISTS(InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError[]) THEN
			SET Environment.Variables.httpReturnCode = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-code";
			SET Environment.Variables.text_out = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-message";
			SET Environment.Variables.stacktrace_out = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-stack";
		ELSE 
			IF Environment.Variables.httpReturnCode is null or CARDINALITY(Environment.Variables.httpReturnCode[]) = 0 THEN
				SET Environment.Variables.httpReturnCode = errorcode;
			END IF;
			-- if an exception is present, try to get a 'formatted' error, else return content
			IF EXISTS(InputExceptionList.*[]) THEN
				call GetProperException(InputExceptionList, Environment.Variables.number_out, Environment.Variables.text_out, Environment.Variables.stacktrace_out);
			ELSE
				SET Environment.Variables.text_out = CAST (InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB AS CHARACTER CCSID InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId); 			
			END IF;
		END IF;
	
	    IF Environment.Variables.httpReturnCode IN ('409', '422') THEN
	    	SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = Environment.Variables.httpReturnCode;
	    ELSE
	    	SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = errorcode;	
	    END IF;
		
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpCode VALUE = Environment.Variables.httpReturnCode;
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpMessage VALUE = Environment.Variables.text_out;
		SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.moreInformation = Environment.Variables.stacktrace_out;

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

	CREATE PROCEDURE CopyMessageHeaders() BEGIN
		DECLARE I INTEGER 1;
		DECLARE J INTEGER;
		SET J = CARDINALITY(InputRoot.*[]);
		WHILE I < J DO
			SET OutputRoot.*[I] = InputRoot.*[I];
			SET I = I + 1;
		END WHILE;
	END;


	CREATE FUNCTION GetProperException(IN InputTree reference,OUT number_out integer, OUT text_out char, OUT stacktrace_out char)
	BEGIN

		Declare STACKTRACE CHARACTER '';
		-- Declare a reference for the ExceptionList
		-- (in a Compute node use InputExceptionList)
		DECLARE start REFERENCE TO InputExceptionList.*[1];
		-- Loop through the exception list children
		WHILE start.Number IS NOT NULL DO
			-- more ESQL
			SET STACKTRACE = CAST(start.Number as CHARACTER) || ':' || COALESCE(start.Text, '') || ':'
			|| COALESCE(start.Label, '') || '
			'|| STACKTRACE ;
			-- Move start to the last child of the field to which it currently points
			MOVE start LASTCHILD;
		END WHILE;
		set text_out=start.Text;
		set stacktrace_out=STACKTRACE;
		set number_out=start.Number;

	END;

END MODULE;

================================================================================

File directory: services-integration-shlib-error-functions/apps/SHLIB_CommonErrorFunctions/CommonHelper/ConnectionErrorBuild.esql

BROKER SCHEMA CommonHelper


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE ConnectionErrorBuild
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		
		IF Exists(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader.[]) THEN 
			IF COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code", '') <> '' THEN
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-code" = InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code";
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-message" = CAST (InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB AS CHARACTER CCSID InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId); 
			END IF;
		ELSE
			THROW USER EXCEPTION MESSAGE 3504 VALUES (InputRoot.[<]); 
		END IF;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;


END MODULE;

================================================================================

File directory: services-integration-shlib-error-functions/apps/SHLIB_CommonErrorFunctions/CommonHelper/ExceptionErrorBuild.esql

BROKER SCHEMA CommonHelper


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE ExceptionErrorBuild
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;

		-- exception from stack trace		
		IF EXISTS(InputExceptionList.*[]) THEN
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-code" = '3508';
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-message" = CAST (InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB AS CHARACTER CCSID InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId); 
			call GetProperException(InputExceptionList) INTO Environment.Variables.OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-stack";

		-- TranslationException
		ELSEIF Exists(InputRoot.JSON.Data.Code[]) THEN 
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-code" = InputRoot.JSON.Data.Code;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-message" = InputRoot.JSON.Data.Message; 
			call GetProperException(InputExceptionList) INTO Environment.Variables.OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-stack";

		-- Error from request	
		ELSEIF Exists(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader.[]) THEN 
			IF COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code", '') <> '' THEN
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-code" = InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code";
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSError."error-message" = CAST (InputRoot.BLOB.BLOB AS CHARACTER CCSID InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId); 
			END IF;
		ELSE
			THROW USER EXCEPTION MESSAGE 3506 VALUES (InputRoot.[<]); 
		END IF;

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;

	CREATE FUNCTION GetProperException(IN InputTree REFERENCE) RETURNS CHAR 
	BEGIN

		DECLARE traceText CHARACTER '';
		-- Declare a reference for the ExceptionList
		-- (in a Compute node use InputExceptionList)
		DECLARE start REFERENCE TO InputExceptionList.*[1];
		-- Loop through the exception list children
		WHILE start.Number IS NOT NULL DO
			-- more ESQL
			SET traceText = CAST(start.Number as CHARACTER) || ' : ' || COALESCE(start.Text, '') || ':'
			|| COALESCE(start.Label, '') || '
			'|| traceText ;
			-- Move start to the last child of the field to which it currently points
			MOVE start LASTCHILD;
		END WHILE;
		
		RETURN traceText;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: services-integration-shlib-error-functions/apps/SHLIB_CommonErrorFunctions/CommonHelper/MonitoringHelper.esql

BROKER SCHEMA CommonHelper


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE SetMonitoringContext
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		
		--Set variables in Env to trace in SUP Log
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller" = InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.URI;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url-parameters" = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Parameters;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url-method" = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Method;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url-path" = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Path;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."correlation-id" = InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."global-transaction-id" = InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Global-Transaction-Id";

		-- add business infos
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers"."case-id" = InputRoot.JSON.Data.eventData.bridgeCase.fullCase.referenceNb;
		IF EXISTS(InputRoot.JSON.Data.eventData.bridgeService[]) THEN
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers"."service-id" = InputRoot.JSON.Data.eventData.bridgeService.id;
		END IF;
		DECLARE allItems CHARACTER;
		CALL CommonHelper.transformListToStringConcatWithSeparator(InputRoot.JSON.Data.naturesOfChanges) INTO allItems;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers"."natures-of-change" = allItems; 
		CALL CommonHelper.transformListToStringConcatWithSeparator(InputRoot.JSON.Data.contextsOfEvent) INTO allItems;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers"."context-of-event" = allItems; 
		
		 
		-- copy all in the LocalEnv as it is passed to the callable flow
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context = Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers" = Environment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers";
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;


END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE EndMonitoringContext
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		CALL setHeadersReply(OutputRoot, Environment);
				
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;


END MODULE;


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE EndFlowAndReturnEmptyPayloadWithContext
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		CALL setHeadersReply(OutputRoot, Environment);
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;


END MODULE;

CREATE PROCEDURE setHeadersReply(IN outMessage REFERENCE, IN environt REFERENCE)
BEGIN
	IF NOT EXISTS (outMessage.HTTPReplyHeader[]) THEN
		IF EXISTS (outMessage.JSON[]) THEN
			CREATE PREVIOUSSIBLING OF outMessage.JSON NAME 'HTTPReplyHeader';
		ELSE
			CREATE PREVIOUSSIBLING OF outMessage.BLOB NAME 'HTTPReplyHeader';
		END IF;
	END IF;
    SET outMessage.HTTPReplyHeader."Content-Type" = 'application/json';
	SET outMessage.HTTPReplyHeader."X-Correlation-ID" =  environt.Variables.TCSLog.context."correlation-id";
	SET outMessage.HTTPReplyHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code" = environt.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller";
	SET outMessage.HTTPReplyHeader."X-Caller-Code" = environt.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code";
	SET outMessage.HTTPReplyHeader."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = environt.Variables.TCSLog.context."global-transaction-id";
END;

CREATE FUNCTION transformListToStringConcatWithSeparator (IN listOfItems REFERENCE) RETURNS CHARACTER
BEGIN
	DECLARE stringList CHARACTER;
	SET stringList = '';
	
	IF EXISTS(listOfItems.[]) THEN
		FOR itm AS listOfItems.Item[] DO
			SET stringList = stringList || ';' || COALESCE(itm);	
		END FOR;
	END IF;
	
	RETURN stringList;				
END;

	CREATE FUNCTION transformStringToListDefinedSeparator (IN inputString CHAR, in separator CHAR, IN RefResult REFERENCE, IN ReturnEmptyValue BOOLEAN) 
	BEGIN
		DECLARE currentIndex INTEGER 1;
		DECLARE element CHARACTER;
		IF POSITION(separator IN inputString) = 0 THEN
		    -- No separator found, add the entire string as a single element
		    if ReturnEmptyValue then
			    	CREATE LASTCHILD OF RefResult name 'Item' VALUE inputString;
			    else
			    	if length(element) > 0 then
			    		CREATE LASTCHILD OF RefResult name 'Item' VALUE inputString;
			    	end if;
			    end if;
		    
		ELSE
			x : WHILE currentIndex <= LENGTH(inputString) DO
				declare toto CHAR SUBSTRING(inputString FROM currentIndex);
				declare tutu int POSITION(separator IN toto);
			    SET element = SUBSTRING(inputString FROM currentIndex FOR POSITION(separator IN SUBSTRING(inputString FROM currentIndex)) - 1);
			    -- Add element to JSON array
			    if ReturnEmptyValue then
			    	CREATE LASTCHILD OF RefResult name 'Item' VALUE element;
			    else
			    	if length(element) > 0 then
			    		CREATE LASTCHILD OF RefResult name 'Item' VALUE element;
			    	end if;
			    end if;
			
			    -- Go to next item
			    SET currentIndex = currentIndex + POSITION(separator IN SUBSTRING(inputString FROM currentIndex));
			    IF POSITION(separator IN SUBSTRING(inputString FROM currentIndex)) = 0 THEN
			        -- Last element (no separator found)
			        SET element = SUBSTRING(inputString FROM currentIndex);
			        if ReturnEmptyValue then
				    	CREATE LASTCHILD OF RefResult name 'Item' VALUE element;
				    else
				    	if length(element) > 0 then
				    		CREATE LASTCHILD OF RefResult name 'Item' VALUE element;
				    	end if;
				    end if;
			        LEAVE x; 
			    END IF;
			END WHILE;
		end if;
	END;

File directory: services-integration-shlib-error-functions/apps/SHLIB_CommonErrorFunctions/CommonHelper/SetMonitoringEnvContextRestApi.esql

BROKER SCHEMA CommonHelper


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE SetMonitoringEnvContextRestApi
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		-- generate contextual id for tracking
		SET Environment.Variables.trackID = UUIDASCHAR;				
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Global-Transaction-Id",'');
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id" = COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Correlation-Id",Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id");	
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code" = COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Caller-Code",'');
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code" = COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code",COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Caller-Code",''));
		SET Environment.Variables."REST-URI" = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.URI;
		SET Environment.Variables."REST-Operation" = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Operation;
			
		-- set contextual variables to track them in SUP logs		
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."rest-operation" = Environment.Variables."REST-Operation";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller" = Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.URI;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."correlation-id" = Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."global-transaction-id" = Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id";	
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05737'; -- APIC
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541'; -- ACE		
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVED ' || InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Operation || ' operation from ' || Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code";			
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-encapsulate-calls/apps/common-integration-shlib-encapsulate-calls/common_integration_shlib_encapsulate_calls/callable_call.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common_integration_shlib_encapsulate_calls

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_callable_Failure
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		DECLARE messageText CHARACTER;
		DECLARE messageCode INTEGER;
		CALL getFormatErrorExceptionDetail(InputExceptionList, messageCode, messageText);
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		IF Environment.Variables.TypeCall = 'async' THEN
			IF NOT EXISTS(OutputRoot.Properties[])
			THEN
				CREATE FIRSTCHILD of OutputRoot domain 'Properties' NAME 'Properties';
			END IF;
			IF Environment.Variables.SetContext IS UNKNOWN AND Environment.Variables.SetContextCallable IS UNKNOWN THEN
				SET Environment.Variables = Environment.CallableFlow.UserContext.Environment.Variables;
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment = Environment.CallableFlow.UserContext.LocalEnvironment;
			ELSE
				SET Environment = InputLocalEnvironment.Environment;
			END IF;
		END IF;
		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;
		SET Environment.Variables.Error."error-code" = messageCode;
		SET Environment.Variables.Error."error-message" = messageText;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'ERROR';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code" = messageCode;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message" = messageText;
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVE Callable response Failure  (' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName|| ')';
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 1 DELETE NONE;

		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code";
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message";
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'FAIL: Callable flow request failure exeption (' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName|| ')';

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_callable_async_call_Failure_Request
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		DECLARE messageText CHARACTER;
		DECLARE messageCode INTEGER;
		CALL getFormatErrorExceptionDetail(InputExceptionList, messageCode, messageText);
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code" = messageCode;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message" = messageText;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'ERROR';
		SET Environment.Variables.Error."error-code" = messageCode;
		SET Environment.Variables.Error."error-message" = messageText;

		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVE Callable Flow request Failure (' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName || ')';
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 1 DELETE NONE;

        SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code";
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message";
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'FAIL: Callable flow request failure exeption (' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName|| ')';
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_callable_Out
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		IF Environment.Variables.TypeCall = 'async' THEN
			SET Environment = InputLocalEnvironment.Environment;
		END IF;		
		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVE Callable response from ' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName;
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 1 DELETE NONE;
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code";
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message";
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUCCESS: Callable request (' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_callable_async_call_SetLogMonitoring
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		IF Environment.Variables.TypeCall NOT IN ('async','') THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('please use ''sync'' or ''async'' for type-call parameter');
		END IF;
		IF not(COALESCE(Environment.Variables.SetContext,false)) THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('please add subflow cc_*.*_start.subflow at the beginning of your flow');
		END IF;
		IF cast(COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment.Destination.CallableFlow.Invoke.requestTimeout,-1) AS INT DEFAULT -1) = -1 THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('please provide requestTimeout in LocalEnvironment.Destination.CallableFlow.Invoke.requestTimeout');
		END IF;
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Correlation-Id" = Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id";
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Origin-Caller-Code" = Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code";		
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Caller-Code" = ApplicationLabel;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Source-System" = 'CMP05541'; -- ACE	
		
		-- set infos variables
		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;
		DELETE FIELD variablesRef.Info;		
		SET variablesRef.Info.targetApplication = OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.CallableFlow.Invoke.targetApplication;
		SET variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName = OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.CallableFlow.Invoke.targetEndpointName;
		SET variablesRef.Info."X-Caller-Code" = ApplicationLabel;
		
		-- set contextual variables
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541';
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541';
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = ApplicationLabel;
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context.targetApplication = variablesRef.Info.targetApplication;
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context.targetEndpointName = variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName;
		
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessageInfo = 'INFO: SEND ' || Environment.Variables.TypeCall || ' request to callable ' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName;
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessageSup = 'SUP: SEND ' || Environment.Variables.TypeCall || ' request to callable ' || variablesRef.Info.targetApplication || ' - ' || variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName;

		SET Environment.CallableFlow.UserContext.Environment.Variables = Environment.Variables;
		SET Environment.CallableFlow.UserContext.LocalEnvironment = OutputLocalEnvironment;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Environment = Environment;
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_callable_end_ExitPoint
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Environment = Environment;
		IF NOT(COALESCE(Environment.Variables.SetContextCallable,FALSE)) THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('please add subflow cc_*.*_start.subflow at the beginning of your flow');
		END IF;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Correlation-ID" =  Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Origin-Caller-Code" = Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code";
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Caller-Code" = ApplicationLabel;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id";
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: SEND response to ' || Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code-Callable";
				
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_callable_start_SetMonitoringEnvContextCallable
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET Environment = InputLocalEnvironment.Environment;
		SET Environment.Variables."cmdb-id-source" = "cmdb-id-source" ;
		-- generate contextual id for tracking
		SET Environment.Variables.trackID = UUIDASCHAR;				
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment."X-Global-Transaction-Id",'');
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment."X-Correlation-Id",Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id");	
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment."X-Caller-Code",'');
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code-Callable" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment."X-Caller-Code",'');
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment."X-Origin-Caller-Code",COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Caller-Code",''));
		SET Environment.Variables."Callable-Application" = InputLocalEnvironment.Destination.CallableFlow.Invoke.targetApplication || ' - ' || InputLocalEnvironment.Destination.CallableFlow.Invoke.targetEndpointName;
			
		-- set contextual variables to track them in SUP logs		
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."callable-application" = Environment.Variables."Callable-Application";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller" = Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."correlation-id" = Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."global-transaction-id" = Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id";	
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541'; -- ACE
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541'; -- ACE
		SET Environment.Variables.SetContextCallable = TRUE;	
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVE Callable request from ' || Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code";		
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

            <eventFilter dataLocation="true()" isXPath="true"/>
          </eventXMLDoc>
        </monitorEvents>
        <monitorEvents eventSourceDescription="Out terminal" eventSourceName="pubNoMatch.terminal.out" eventIdentity="$Environment/Variables/MonitoringMessage">

File directory: common-integration-shlib-encapsulate-calls/apps/common-integration-shlib-encapsulate-calls/common_integration_shlib_encapsulate_calls/http_call.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common_integration_shlib_encapsulate_calls
DECLARE "monitoring-api-name" EXTERNAL CHAR '';
DECLARE "monitoring-api-operation" EXTERNAL CHAR '';
DECLARE "provider-name" EXTERNAL CHAR '';
--DECLARE "cmdb-id-source" EXTERNAL CHAR 'default';
DECLARE "cmdb-id-target" EXTERNAL CHAR 'default';

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_http_call_SetLogMonitoring
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		IF LENGTH("monitoring-api-name") = 0 THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('please provide "monitoring-api-name" on subflow cc_http_async_call.subflow');
		END IF;
		IF LENGTH("monitoring-api-operation") = 0 THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('please provide "monitoring-api-operation" on subflow cc_http_async_call.subflow');
		END IF;
		IF not(COALESCE(Environment.Variables.SetContext,false)) THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('please add subflow cc_*.*_start.subflow at the beginning of your flow');
		END IF;
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		DECLARE RefHTTPRequestHeader REFERENCE to OutputRoot;
		IF EXISTS(OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader[]) THEN
			MOVE RefHTTPRequestHeader to OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader;
		ELSE
			CREATE NEXTSIBLING OF OutputRoot.Properties as RefHTTPRequestHeader NAME 'HTTPRequestHeader';
		END IF;
		
		SET RefHTTPRequestHeader."X-Correlation-Id" = Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET RefHTTPRequestHeader."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id";
		SET RefHTTPRequestHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code" = Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code";		
		SET RefHTTPRequestHeader."X-Caller-Code" = ApplicationLabel;
		SET RefHTTPRequestHeader."X-Source-System" =  'CMP05541'; -- ACE	
		SET RefHTTPRequestHeader."X-Target-System" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment."X-Target-System", 'CMP05737');--APIC
		
		-- set infos variables
		DECLARE variablesRef REFERENCE TO Environment.Variables;
		DELETE FIELD variablesRef.Info;		
		SET variablesRef.Info.HTTPMethod = InputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestLine.Method;
		SET variablesRef.Info.RequestURL = InputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL;
		SET variablesRef.Info."X-Caller-Code" = InputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."X-Caller-Code";
		SET variablesRef.Info."OperationType" = "monitoring-api-operation";
		SET variablesRef.Info.targetApplication = variablesRef.TCSLog.context.targetApplication;
		SET variablesRef.Info.targetEndpointName = variablesRef.TCSLog.context.targetEndpointName;
		
		-- set contextual variables
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context."source-system" = OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."X-Source-System";
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context."target-system" = OutputRoot.HTTPRequestHeader."X-Target-System";
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = ApplicationLabel;
		SET variablesRef.TCSLog.context."target-url" = InputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.RequestURL;
		
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessageInfo = 'INFO: SEND ' || Environment.Variables.TypeCall ||' request to ' || "monitoring-api-name";
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessageSup = 'SUP: SEND ' || Environment.Variables.TypeCall ||' request to ' || "monitoring-api-name";
		
		-- prepare context for async 
		IF Environment.Variables.TypeCall = 'async' THEN
			DECLARE Context ROW;
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF Context DOMAIN('JSON') NAME 'JSON';
			SET Context.JSON.Data.Environment = Environment;
			SET Context.JSON.Data.LocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
			SET Context.BLOB = ASBITSTREAM(Context.JSON.Data OPTIONS FolderBitStream CCSID 1208);
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
			SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.UserContext = Context.BLOB;
		END IF;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;
CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_http_async_call_Catch
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET Environment.Variables.TypeCall = 'async';
		CALL GetContext(InputLocalEnvironment.HTTP.Response.UserContext, Environment,OutputLocalEnvironment,InputLocalEnvironment);
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_http_call_Error
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		--set OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		IF Environment.Variables.TypeCall = 'async' THEN
			CALL GetContext(InputLocalEnvironment.HTTP.Response.UserContext, Environment,OutputLocalEnvironment,InputLocalEnvironment);
		END IF;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541'; --ACE
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment."X-Target-System", 'CMP05737'); --APIC	
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'ERROR';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code"    = InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message" = InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Line";		
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVE HTTP response error from ' || "monitoring-api-name";
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 1 DELETE NONE;
		--check if cmdbid are referenced
		IF Environment.Variables."cmdb-id-source" <> 'default' AND "cmdb-id-target" <> 'default' THEN
			SET Environment.Variables.ContextCode = Environment.Variables."cmdb-id-source" || '_' || "cmdb-id-target" || '_' || CAST(InputRoot.HTTPResponseHeader."X-Original-HTTP-Status-Code" AS CHARACTER);
		END IF;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code";
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message";
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'ERROR: HTTP error generated  from ' || "monitoring-api-name" || ' - ' || "monitoring-api-operation";	
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_http_call_Failure
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		--set OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		DECLARE messageText CHARACTER;
		DECLARE messageCode INTEGER;
		CALL getFormatErrorExceptionDetail(InputExceptionList, messageCode, messageText);
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		IF Environment.Variables.TypeCall = 'async' THEN
			call GetContext(InputLocalEnvironment.HTTP.Response.UserContext, Environment,OutputLocalEnvironment,InputLocalEnvironment);
		END IF;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541'; --ACE
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment."X-Target-System", 'CMP05737'); --APIC
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'ERROR';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code"    = messageCode;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message" = messageText;		
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVE HTTP response Failure from ' || "monitoring-api-name";
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 1 DELETE NONE;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code";
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message";
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'FAIL: HTTP failure exception when calling ' || "monitoring-api-name" || ' - ' || "monitoring-api-operation";
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_http_async_call_Failure_Request
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		DECLARE messageText CHARACTER;
		DECLARE messageCode INTEGER;
		CALL getFormatErrorExceptionDetail(InputExceptionList, messageCode, messageText);
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541'; --ACE
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = COALESCE(OutputLocalEnvironment."X-Target-System", 'CMP05737');--APIC
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'ERROR';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code"    = messageCode;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message" = messageText;
		SET Environment.Variables.Error."error-code"    = messageCode;
		SET Environment.Variables.Error."error-message" = messageText;		
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVE HTTP response Failure from ' || "monitoring-api-name";
		
		
		PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 1 DELETE NONE;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code";
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message";
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'FAIL: HTTP failure exeption when calling ' || "monitoring-api-name" || ' - ' || "monitoring-api-operation";
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_http_call_Out
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		--set OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		IF Environment.Variables.TypeCall = 'async' THEN
			CALL GetContext(InputLocalEnvironment.HTTP.Response.UserContext, Environment,OutputLocalEnvironment,InputLocalEnvironment);
		END IF;
		DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code";
        DELETE FIELD Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05541'; --ACE
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment."X-Target-System", 'CMP05737'); --APIC
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVE HTTP response ok from ' || "monitoring-api-name";
		PROPAGATE TO terminal 1 DELETE NONE;
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUCCESS: HTTP request ' || "monitoring-api-name" || ' - ' || "monitoring-api-operation";
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_http_async_call_type_call_async
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET Environment.Variables.TypeCall = 'async';
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET Environment.Variables.TypeCall = 'sync';
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-encapsulate-calls/apps/common-integration-shlib-encapsulate-calls/common_integration_shlib_encapsulate_calls/mq.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common_integration_shlib_encapsulate_calls
DECLARE MQReply EXTERNAL BOOLEAN FALSE;
DECLARE BackoutThreshold EXTERNAL CHARACTER '';
DECLARE "cmdb-id-source" EXTERNAL CHAR 'default';
DECLARE queueName EXTERNAL CHAR '';


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_app_mq_start_SetMonitoringEnvContextAppMq
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET Environment.Variables."cmdb-id-source" = "cmdb-id-source" ;
		SET Environment.Variables.BackoutCount = InputRoot.MQMD.BackoutCount;
		SET Environment.Variables.BackoutThreshold = CAST(BackoutThreshold as INTEGER);
		IF Environment.Variables.BackoutCount + 1 >= Environment.Variables.BackoutThreshold THEN
			SET Environment.Variables.LastTry = TRUE;
		ELSE
			SET Environment.Variables.LastTry = FALSE;
		END IF;
		-- generate contextual id for tracking
		SET Environment.Variables.trackID = UUIDASCHAR;
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = COALESCE(InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.gtid,'');
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id" = COALESCE(InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.correlationId,COALESCE(Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id",''));
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code" = COALESCE(InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.callerCode,'');
		SET Environment.Variables.In."X-Caller-Code" = COALESCE(InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.callerCode,'');
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code" = COALESCE(InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.originCallerCode,'');
		SET Environment.Variables.SourceQueue = InputRoot.MQMD.SourceQueue;
		SET Environment.Variables.Topic = InputRoot.MQRFH2.mqps.Top;
		-- set contextual variables to track them in SUP logs
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-queue" = Environment.Variables.SourceQueue;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."mq-topic" = Environment.Variables.Topic;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller" = Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."correlation-id" = Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."global-transaction-id" = Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05625'; -- MQ
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541'; -- ACE
		SET Environment.Variables.SetContext = true;
		if EXISTS(Environment.Variables.Topic[]) then
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = Environment.Variables.Topic;
			SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVED message from queue ' || Environment.Variables.SourceQueue || ' through topic ' || Environment.Variables.Topic;
			SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessageCatch = 'FAIL: RECEIVED Catch message from queue ' || Environment.Variables.SourceQueue || ' through topic ' || Environment.Variables.Topic;
		else
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = Environment.Variables.SourceQueue;
			SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVED message from queue ' || Environment.Variables.SourceQueue ;
			SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessageCatch = 'FAIL: RECEIVED Catch message from queue ' || Environment.Variables.SourceQueue;
		end if;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;
CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_app_mq_end_ExitPoint
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot.Properties = InputRoot.Properties ;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		IF NOT(COALESCE(Environment.Variables.SetContext,false)) THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('please add subflow cc_*.*_start.subflow at the beginning of your flow');
		END IF;
		IF EXISTS(InputExceptionList.*[]) THEN
			DECLARE messageText CHARACTER;
			DECLARE messageCode INTEGER;
			CALL getFormatErrorExceptionDetail(InputExceptionList, messageCode, messageText);
			SET Environment.Variables.ContextCode = CAST(messageCode AS CHARACTER);
		END IF;
		
		IF Environment.Variables.ContextCode IS NOT NULL AND Environment.Variables.ContextCode <> '' AND EXISTS(Environment.Variables.MessageIn.*[]) THEN
			--check if records needed
			SET Environment.Variables.RECORD = THE(SELECT ITEM t.CONTEXT_RECORD 
												   FROM Database.ACE_CONTEXTREFERENCE_DB.dbo.CONTEXTREFERENCE AS t 
												   WHERE t.CONTEXT_CODE = Environment.Variables.ContextCode) ;
			IF Environment.Variables.RECORD THEN
				PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out2' DELETE NONE;
			END IF;
		END IF;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = Environment.Variables.In."X-Caller-Code" ;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = 'CMP05625'; --MQ
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541'; --ACE
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: SEND transaction finished ';
		IF EXISTS(Environment.Variables.Topic[]) THEN
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = Environment.Variables.Topic;
		ELSE
			SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = Environment.Variables.SourceQueue;
		END IF;
		IF MQReply AND Environment.Variables.ReplyToQ IS NOT NULL THEN
			DELETE FIELD OutputRoot.Properties;
			FOR Field AS Environment.Variables.MessageIn.*[] DO
				if not (FIELDNAME(Field) = 'BLOB' or FIELDNAME(Field) = 'JSON' or FIELDNAME(Field) = 'XMLNSC' or FIELDNAME(Field) = 'DFDL') then
					CREATE LASTCHILD OF OutputRoot DOMAIN(FIELDNAME(Field)) NAME FIELDNAME(Field);
					SET OutputRoot.{FIELDNAME(Field)} = Field;
				end if;
			END FOR;
			FOR Field AS InputRoot.*[] DO
				if FIELDNAME(Field) = 'BLOB' or FIELDNAME(Field) = 'JSON' or FIELDNAME(Field) = 'XMLNSC' or FIELDNAME(Field) = 'DFDL' then 
					CREATE LASTCHILD OF OutputRoot DOMAIN(FIELDNAME(Field)) NAME FIELDNAME(Field);
					SET OutputRoot.{FIELDNAME(Field)} = Field;
				end if;
			END FOR;						
			SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: SEND transaction finished to ' || Environment.Variables.ReplyToQ;
			SET OutputRoot.MQMD.ReplyToQ = Environment.Variables.ReplyToQ;
			PROPAGATE TO TERMINAL 'out1' DELETE NONE;
		END IF;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_rest_api_start_SetMonitoringEnvContextRestApi
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot = InputRoot;
		SET Environment.Variables."cmdb-id-source" = "cmdb-id-source" ;
		-- generate contextual id for tracking
		SET Environment.Variables.trackID = UUIDASCHAR;				
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Global-Transaction-Id",'');
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id" = COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Correlation-Id",Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id");	
		SET Environment.Variables.In."target-url" = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.URI,'');
		SET Environment.Variables.In."X-Caller-Code" = COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Caller-Code",'');SET Environment.Variables."X-Caller-Code" = COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Caller-Code",'');
		SET Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code" = COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code",COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Caller-Code",''));
		SET Environment.Variables.In."X-Source-System" = COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Source-System", 'CMP05737');
		SET Environment.Variables."REST-URI" = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.URI;
		SET Environment.Variables."REST-QueryParamsString" = InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Query-String";
		SET Environment.Variables."REST-Operation" = InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Operation;
			
		-- set contextual variables to track them in SUP logs		
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context.severity = 'INFO';
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."rest-operation" = Environment.Variables."REST-Operation";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller" = Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = Environment.Variables.In."X-Caller-Code";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = Environment.Variables.In."target-url";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."correlation-id" = Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."global-transaction-id" = Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id";	
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Source-System", 'CMP05737');--APIC
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541'; --ACE
		SET Environment.Variables.SetContext = true;	
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: RECEIVE ' || InputLocalEnvironment.REST.Input.Operation || ' operation from ' || Environment.Variables.In."X-Caller-Code";			
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE cc_rest_api_end_ExitPoint
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot.Properties = InputRoot.Properties;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment = InputLocalEnvironment;
		IF NOT(COALESCE(Environment.Variables.SetContext,FALSE)) THEN
			THROW USER EXCEPTION VALUES('please add subflow cc_*.*_start.subflow at the beginning of your flow');
		END IF;		
		--check if records needed
		IF Environment.Variables.ContextCode IS NOT NULL AND Environment.Variables.ContextCode <> '' THEN
			SET Environment.Variables.OVERRIDE = THE(SELECT ITEM t.CONTEXT_OVERRIDE FROM Database.ACE_CONTEXTREFERENCE_DB.dbo.CONTEXTREFERENCE AS t WHERE t.CONTEXT_CODE = Environment.Variables.ContextCode AND t.CONTEXT_LANGUAGE = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment.Context.Language,'EN'));
			IF Environment.Variables.OVERRIDE THEN
				SET Environment.Variables.CONTEXT[] = SELECT t.CONTEXT_STATUS_CODE, t.CONTEXT_STATUS_MESSAGE   
													  FROM Database.ACE_CONTEXTREFERENCE_DB.dbo.CONTEXTREFERENCE AS t 
													  WHERE t.CONTEXT_CODE = Environment.Variables.ContextCode AND t.CONTEXT_LANGUAGE = COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment.Context.Language,'EN');
			
				SET OutputRoot.HTTPReplyHeader."Content-Type" = 'application/json; charset=utf-8';
				SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.refAppError = Environment.Variables.ContextCode;
				SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpCode = Environment.Variables.CONTEXT.CONTEXT_STATUS_CODE;
				SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpMessage = Environment.Variables.CONTEXT.CONTEXT_STATUS_MESSAGE;
				SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.moreInformation = Environment.Variables.Info.moreInformation;
				SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.timestamp = CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP as CHAR FORMAT 'I');
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = Environment.Variables.CONTEXT.CONTEXT_STATUS_CODE;
				
				SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-code" = Environment.Variables.CONTEXT.CONTEXT_STATUS_CODE;
				SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."error-message" = OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpMessage;
				SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."context-code" = Environment.Variables.ContextCode ; 
			ELSEIF Environment.Variables.OVERRIDE IS NULL THEN
				SET OutputRoot.HTTPReplyHeader."Content-Type" = 'application/json; charset=utf-8';
				SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.refAppError = Environment.Variables.ContextCode;
				SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpCode = 500;
				SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.httpMessage = 'No value provided in database for this ContextCode: ' || Environment.Variables.ContextCode || ' and Language: ' || COALESCE(InputLocalEnvironment.Context.Language,'EN');
				SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.moreInformation = Environment.Variables.Info.moreInformation;
				SET OutputRoot.JSON.Data.timestamp = CAST(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP as CHAR FORMAT 'I');
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Destination.HTTP.ReplyStatusCode = 500;
			ELSE
				
				SET OutputRoot = InputRoot ;
			END IF;
		ELSE
			SET OutputRoot = InputRoot ;
		END IF;
		
		DECLARE RefHTTPReplyHeader REFERENCE to OutputRoot;
		IF EXISTS(OutputRoot.HTTPReplyHeader[]) THEN
			MOVE RefHTTPReplyHeader to OutputRoot.HTTPReplyHeader;
		ELSE
			CREATE NEXTSIBLING OF OutputRoot.Properties as RefHTTPReplyHeader NAME 'HTTPReplyHeader';
		END IF;
		
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = Environment.Variables.In."X-Caller-Code" ;
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-url" = Environment.Variables.In."target-url";
	    SET RefHTTPReplyHeader."X-Correlation-ID" =  Environment.Variables."X-Correlation-Id";
		SET RefHTTPReplyHeader."X-Origin-Caller-Code" = Environment.Variables."X-Origin-Caller-Code";
		SET RefHTTPReplyHeader."X-Caller-Code" = ApplicationLabel;
		SET RefHTTPReplyHeader."X-Global-Transaction-Id" = Environment.Variables."X-Global-Transaction-Id";
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = COALESCE(InputRoot.HTTPInputHeader."X-Source-System", 'CMP05737');--APIC
		SET Environment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = 'CMP05541'; --ACE
		SET Environment.Variables.MonitoringMessage = 'SUP: SEND response to ' || Environment.Variables.In."X-Caller-Code";

		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-encapsulate-calls/apps/common-integration-shlib-encapsulate-calls/common_integration_shlib_encapsulate_calls/Utils.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common_integration_shlib_encapsulate_calls

CREATE PROCEDURE GetContext(IN UserContext REFERENCE,IN Environment REFERENCE, IN OutputLocalEnvironment REFERENCE,IN InputLocalEnvironment REFERENCE ) BEGIN
	DECLARE Context ROW;
	CREATE LASTCHILD OF Context DOMAIN('JSON') PARSE(UserContext);
	SET Environment = Context.JSON.Data.Environment;
	SET OutputLocalEnvironment = Context.JSON.Data.LocalEnvironment;

	FOR BlocUp AS InputLocalEnvironment.*[] DO
		FOR BlocDown AS BlocUp.*[] DO
			FOR Child AS BlocDown.*[] DO
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.{FIELDNAME(BlocUp)}.{FIELDNAME(BlocDown)}.{FIELDNAME(Child)} = Child;
			END FOR;
		END FOR;
	END FOR;
END;

CREATE PROCEDURE getFormatErrorExceptionDetail(IN InputTree REFERENCE,OUT messageNumber INTEGER, OUT messageText CHARACTER)
BEGIN
	-- Create a reference to the first child of the exception list
	DECLARE ptrException REFERENCE TO InputTree.*[1];
	-- keep looping while the moves to the child of exception list work
	WHILE lastmove(ptrException) DO
		-- store the current values for the error number and text
		IF ptrException.Number IS NOT NULL THEN
			SET messageNumber = ptrException.Number;
			SET messageText = ptrException.Text||': ';

			DECLARE i INTEGER 0;
			WHILE ptrException.Insert[i+1].Text IS NOT NULL DO
				SET messageText = messageText || ptrException.Insert[i+1].Text;
				SET i = i+1;
			END WHILE;
		END IF;
		-- now move to the last child which should be the next exceptionlist
		MOVE ptrException LASTCHILD;
	END WHILE;
END;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-wrap-message/apps/common-integration-shlib-wrap-message/common_integration_shlib_wrap_message/messageBuild.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common_integration_shlib_wrap_message


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE messageBuild
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId = InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId;
		SET OutputRoot.Properties.Encoding = InputRoot.Properties.Encoding;
		SET OutputRoot.Properties.Topic = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.Topic;
		
		CREATE LASTCHILD OF OutputRoot DOMAIN('MQMD') NAME 'MQMD';
		SET OutputRoot.MQMD.Format 							= MQFMT_NONE;
		SET OutputRoot.MQMD.CodedCharSetId 					= COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId, 1208);
		SET OutputRoot.MQMD.Encoding 						= COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.Encoding, 546);
		SET OutputRoot.MQMD.ReplyToQ 						= Environment.Variables.SourceQueue;
		CREATE NEXTSIBLING OF OutputRoot.MQMD DOMAIN ('MQRFH2') NAME 'MQRFH2';	
		
		--set default header with properties values
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.(MQRFH2.Field)Version = MQRFH_VERSION_2;
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.(MQRFH2.Field)Encoding = COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.Encoding, 546);
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.(MQRFH2.Field)CodedCharSetId = COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId, 1208);
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.(MQRFH2.Field)Format = MQFMT_STRING; 
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.(MQRFH2.Field)NameValueCCSID = COALESCE(InputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId, 1208);
		--keep message to propagate
		SET OutputRoot.{FIELDNAME(InputRoot.*[<])} = InputRoot.{FIELDNAME(InputRoot.*[<])};
		--Set specific header
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.gtid = COALESCE(Environment.Monitoring.EventCorrelation.globalTransactionId,InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.gtid) ; 
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.ptid = COALESCE(Environment.Monitoring.EventCorrelation.parentTransactionId,InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.tid) ; 
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.ltid = COALESCE(Environment.Monitoring.EventCorrelation.localTransactionId,InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.cid) ; 
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.callerCode = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code";
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.originCallerCode = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller";
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.sourceSystem = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system";
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.targetSystem = InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system";
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.appName = ApplicationLabel;
		
		--Business identifiers 
		DECLARE businessIdentifiersRef REFERENCE TO InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers".[1];
		WHILE LASTMOVE(businessIdentifiersRef) DO
			SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.{BUSINESSIDENTIFIERSEPARATOR||FIELDNAME(businessIdentifiersRef)} =  FIELDVALUE(businessIdentifiersRef);
			MOVE businessIdentifiersRef NEXTSIBLING;
		END WHILE;
		
		--User Defined 
		DECLARE userDefinedRef REFERENCE TO InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.userDefined.[1];
		WHILE LASTMOVE(userDefinedRef) DO
			SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.{USERDEFINEDSEPARATOR||FIELDNAME(userDefinedRef)} =  FIELDVALUE(userDefinedRef);
			MOVE userDefinedRef NEXTSIBLING;
		END WHILE;
		
		-- filters
		DECLARE filtersRef REFERENCE TO InputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.filters.[1] ;
		WHILE LASTMOVE(filtersRef) DO
			SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.{FILTERSEPARATOR||FIELDNAME(filtersRef)} =  FIELDVALUE(filtersRef);
			MOVE filtersRef NEXTSIBLING;
		END WHILE;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-wrap-message/apps/common-integration-shlib-wrap-message/common_integration_shlib_wrap_message/messageExtract.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common_integration_shlib_wrap_message
DECLARE BUSINESSIDENTIFIERSEPARATOR CHARACTER 'BI';
DECLARE USERDEFINEDSEPARATOR CHARACTER 'UD';
DECLARE FILTERSEPARATOR CHARACTER 'FT';

CREATE COMPUTE MODULE messageExtract
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		SET OutputRoot.Properties = InputRoot.Properties;
		--get default header with properties values
		SET OutputRoot.Properties.Encoding = InputRoot.MQRFH2.(MQRFH2.Field)Encoding ;
		SET OutputRoot.Properties.CodedCharSetId = InputRoot.MQRFH2.(MQRFH2.Field)CodedCharSetId ;
		--keep message to propagate
		SET OutputRoot.{FIELDNAME(InputRoot.*[<])} = InputRoot.{FIELDNAME(InputRoot.*[<])};
		
		
		--Set specific header
		--Set monitoring ID only for Global transaction ID (=gtid) and Correlation ID (=ptid)
		SET Environment.Monitoring.EventCorrelation.globalTransactionId = InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.gtid ;
		SET Environment.Monitoring.EventCorrelation.parentTransactionId = InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.ptid ;
		--SET Environment.Monitoring.EventCorrelation.localTransactionId = InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.ltid ;
		
		--save all message for record
		SET Environment.Variables.MessageIn = InputRoot ;
				
		--Get specific MQ field
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.Topic = FIELDVALUE(InputRoot.MQRFH2.mqps.Top);
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.SourceQueue = InputRoot.MQMD.SourceQueue;
		SET Environment.Variables.SourceQueue = InputRoot.MQMD.SourceQueue;
		SET Environment.Variables.ReplyToQ = InputRoot.MQMD.ReplyToQ ;
		
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."caller-code" = InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.callerCode ;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."origin-caller" = InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.originCallerCode ;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."source-system" = InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.sourceSystem ;
		SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.context."target-system" = InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.targetSystem ;
				
		--Set in LocalEnvironment Business identifiers and Userdefined saved in headers
		DECLARE mqrfh2Headers REFERENCE TO InputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.[1];
		WHILE LASTMOVE(mqrfh2Headers) DO
			IF STARTSWITH(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers),BUSINESSIDENTIFIERSEPARATOR) THEN
				DECLARE bitmp CHARACTER SUBSTRING(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers) FROM LENGTH(BUSINESSIDENTIFIERSEPARATOR)+1 FOR LENGTH(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers))-LENGTH(BUSINESSIDENTIFIERSEPARATOR));
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog."business-identifiers".{bitmp} = mqrfh2Headers ;
			ELSEIF STARTSWITH(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers),USERDEFINEDSEPARATOR) THEN
				DECLARE udtmp CHARACTER SUBSTRING(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers) FROM LENGTH(USERDEFINEDSEPARATOR)+1 FOR LENGTH(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers))-LENGTH(USERDEFINEDSEPARATOR));
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.userDefined.{udtmp} = mqrfh2Headers ;
			ELSEIF STARTSWITH(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers),FILTERSEPARATOR) THEN
				DECLARE fttmp CHARACTER SUBSTRING(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers) FROM LENGTH(FILTERSEPARATOR)+1 FOR LENGTH(FIELDNAME(mqrfh2Headers))-LENGTH(FILTERSEPARATOR));
				SET OutputLocalEnvironment.Variables.TCSLog.filters.{fttmp} = mqrfh2Headers ;
			END IF;
			MOVE mqrfh2Headers NEXTSIBLING;
		END WHILE;
		
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-wrap-message/apps/common-integration-shlib-wrap-message/common_integration_shlib_wrap_message/messageRecover.esql

BROKER SCHEMA common_integration_shlib_wrap_message
DECLARE runtimeEnvironment EXTERNAL CHARACTER 'DEV';


CREATE COMPUTE MODULE messageRecover
	CREATE FUNCTION Main() RETURNS BOOLEAN
	BEGIN
		
		DELETE FIELD OutputRoot.Properties;
		FOR Field AS Environment.Variables.MessageIn.*[] DO
			CREATE LASTCHILD OF OutputRoot DOMAIN(FIELDNAME(Field)) NAME FIELDNAME(Field);
			SET OutputRoot.{FIELDNAME(Field)} = Field;
		END FOR;
		--define reply queue + app name
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.SourceQueue = OutputRoot.MQMD.SourceQueue ;
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.appReplayName = ApplicationLabel;
		SET OutputRoot.MQRFH2.usr.runtimeEnvironment = runtimeEnvironment;
		RETURN TRUE;
	END;
END MODULE;

File directory: common-integration-shlib-policies-reader/apps/common-integration-shlib-policies-reader/Shlib_PoliciesReader/GetUDPEsqlFunction.esql

BROKER SCHEMA Shlib_PoliciesReader

CREATE FUNCTION gestionPolicy( IN policyName CHARACTER, IN propertyName CHARACTER ) RETURNS CHARACTER
BEGIN
	DECLARE prop CHAR getPolicyPropertyWithOutError(policyName, propertyName);
	if prop = 'POLICY_NOT_FOUND' OR prop = 'PROPERTY_NOT_FOUND' THEN 
		THROW USER EXCEPTION CATALOG 'MyCatalog' MESSAGE 4004 VALUES(policyName || '/' || propertyName || ' not found');
	end if;
	return prop;

END;
CREATE PROCEDURE getAllPolicyProperties (IN PolicyProjectName CHAR,IN PolicyName CHAR, IN rowRef REFERENCE )  
BEGIN
	/* 
		exemple d'appel  
		call getAllPolicyProperties('DigitalIntakePolicies','BALOISEDIG',OutputRoot.JSON.Data.test);
	*/
	DECLARE properties CHAR getAllPolicyPropertiesJava('{' || PolicyProjectName || '}:' ||  PolicyName);
	DECLARE RowTempo ROW;
	DECLARE dataAsBit BIT  CAST(properties AS BIT CCSID 1208);
	CREATE LASTCHILD OF RowTempo DOMAIN 'JSON' PARSE(dataAsBit CCSID 1208);
	set rowRef = RowTempo.JSON.Data;
	
END;
CREATE FUNCTION getPolicyProperty( IN policyName CHARACTER, IN propertyName CHARACTER )
RETURNS CHARACTER
LANGUAGE JAVA
EXTERNAL NAME "GETUDP_JavaCompute.getPolicyProperty";

CREATE FUNCTION getPolicyPropertyWithOutError( IN policyName CHARACTER, IN propertyName CHARACTER )
RETURNS CHARACTER
LANGUAGE JAVA
EXTERNAL NAME "GETUDP_JavaCompute.getPolicyPropertyWithOutError";

CREATE FUNCTION getAllPolicyPropertiesJava( IN policyName CHARACTER )
RETURNS CHARACTER
LANGUAGE JAVA
EXTERNAL NAME "GETUDP_JavaCompute.getAllPolicyProperties";
